template<typename K,typename V>  String * MapBase<K,V>::mapToString(Map<Object *,Object *> * m)// Block

{// IfStatement

  if (// StaticInvocation
isToStringVisiting(// VariableGet
m)) // Block

  {// ReturnStatement

    return // StringLiteral
String::cppNew("{...}",sizeof("{...}"));
  }// VariableDeclaration

  StringBuffer * result = // ConstructorInvocation
StringBuffer::cppCtr_(StringBuffer::cppNew(), // ConstantExpression
String::cppNew("",sizeof("")));// TryFinally

  try{ // Block

  {// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
List<Object *>::add(// StaticGet
, // VariableGet
m);// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // StringLiteral
String::cppNew("{",sizeof("{")));// VariableDeclaration

    Bool * first = // BoolLiteral
Bool::cppNew(true);// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
Map<Object *,Object *>::forEach(// VariableGet
m, // FunctionExpression
new ClosureWrapper<void,void, Object *, Object *>([&](Object * k, Object * v) -> void// Block

    {// IfStatement

      if (// Not
Bool::cpp_not(// VariableGet
first)) // Block

      {// ExpressionStatement

        // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // StringLiteral
String::cppNew(", ",sizeof(", ")));
      }// ExpressionStatement

      // VariableSet
first = // BoolLiteral
Bool::cppNew(false);// ExpressionStatement

      // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // VariableGet
k);// ExpressionStatement

      // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // StringLiteral
String::cppNew(": ",sizeof(": ")));// ExpressionStatement

      // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // VariableGet
v);
    }));// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::write(// VariableGet
result, // StringLiteral
String::cppNew("}",sizeof("}")));
  }}finally {// Block

  {// AssertStatement

    print("assert");// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
List<Object *>::removeLast(// StaticGet
);
  }};// ReturnStatement

  return // InstanceInvocation
StringBuffer::toString(// VariableGet
result);
}

template<typename K,typename V>  void MapBase<K,V>::_fillMapWithMappedIterable(Map<Object *,Object *> * map, Iterable<Object *> * iterable, FunctionApply<Object *,Object, Object *> * key, FunctionApply<Object *,Object, Object *> * value)// Block

{// ExpressionStatement

  // ConditionalExpression
(// EqualsNull
(// VariableGet
key == nullptr)) ? // VariableSet
key = // ConstantExpression
AA<MapBase._id>AA : // NullLiteral
nullptr;// ExpressionStatement

  // ConditionalExpression
(// EqualsNull
(// VariableGet
value == nullptr)) ? // VariableSet
value = // ConstantExpression
AA<MapBase._id>AA : // NullLiteral
nullptr;// Block

  {// VariableDeclaration

    Iterator<Object *> * $sync_for_iterator = // InstanceGet
Iterable<Object *>::cppGet_iterator(// VariableGet
iterable);// ForStatement

    {while (// InstanceInvocation
Iterator<Object *>::moveNext(// VariableGet
$sync_for_iterator)){// Block

        {// VariableDeclaration

          Object * element = // InstanceGet
Iterator<Object *>::cppGet_current(// VariableGet
$sync_for_iterator);// Block

          {// ExpressionStatement

            // InstanceInvocation
Map<Object *,Object *>::cpp_subscriptAssign(// VariableGet
map, // FunctionInvocation
// VariableGet
key(// VariableGet
element), // FunctionInvocation
// VariableGet
value(// VariableGet
element));
          }
        }}}
  }
}

template<typename K,typename V>  void MapBase<K,V>::_fillMapWithIterables(Map<Object *,Object *> * map, Iterable<Object *> * keys, Iterable<Object *> * values)// Block

{// VariableDeclaration

  Iterator<Object *> * keyIterator = // InstanceGet
Iterable<Object *>::cppGet_iterator(// VariableGet
keys);// VariableDeclaration

  Iterator<Object *> * valueIterator = // InstanceGet
Iterable<Object *>::cppGet_iterator(// VariableGet
values);// VariableDeclaration

  Bool * hasNextKey = // InstanceInvocation
Iterator<Object *>::moveNext(// VariableGet
keyIterator);// VariableDeclaration

  Bool * hasNextValue = // InstanceInvocation
Iterator<Object *>::moveNext(// VariableGet
valueIterator);// WhileStatement

  while (// LogicalExpression
// VariableGet
hasNextKey && // VariableGet
hasNextValue) // Block

  {// ExpressionStatement

    // InstanceInvocation
Map<Object *,Object *>::cpp_subscriptAssign(// VariableGet
map, // InstanceGet
Iterator<Object *>::cppGet_current(// VariableGet
keyIterator), // InstanceGet
Iterator<Object *>::cppGet_current(// VariableGet
valueIterator));// ExpressionStatement

    // VariableSet
hasNextKey = // InstanceInvocation
Iterator<Object *>::moveNext(// VariableGet
keyIterator);// ExpressionStatement

    // VariableSet
hasNextValue = // InstanceInvocation
Iterator<Object *>::moveNext(// VariableGet
valueIterator);
  }// IfStatement

  if (// LogicalExpression
// VariableGet
hasNextKey || // VariableGet
hasNextValue) // Block

  {// ExpressionStatement

    // Throw
throw "ConstructorInvocation(new ArgumentError(Iterables do not have same length.))";
  }
}

// ThisExpression
